<cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute Every rank owns its A row block and a full copy of B\n//             (both generated locally from the deterministic generator, so no\n//             broadcast is needed). Row blocks of C are gathered to rank 0.\n//   * CUDA  : each rank drives one GPU (local rank -> device, round-robin).\n//             The local row block is processed in chunks on two streams so\n//             host<->device transfers overlap with the tiled DGEMM kernel.\n//   * OpenMP: multi-threaded matrix initialisation, result packing and\n//             host-side validation on every rank.\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call); OpenMP parallel over rows.\nvoid initMatrixRows(double* mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numRows) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < numRows; ++r) {\n        const size_t i = rowBegin + r;\n        double* row = mat + r * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA tiled DGEMM kernel: C[M x N] = A[M x K] * B[K x N] (row-major)\n// Block tile BM x BN, K-step BK, each thread computes a TM x TN micro tile.\n// Shared memory is double buffered and global loads are prefetched into\n// registers so that loads of tile t+1 overlap the FMAs of tile t.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int BM = 64;\nconstexpr int BN = 64;\nconstexpr int BK = 16;\nconstexpr int TM = 4;\nconstexpr int TN = 4;\nconstexpr int THREADS_X = BN / TN;  // 16\nconstexpr int THREADS_Y = BM / TM;  // 16\nconstexpr int NTHREADS = THREADS_X * THREADS_Y;  // 256\nconstexpr int A_LOADS_PER_THREAD = (BM * BK) / NTHREADS;  // 4\nconstexpr int B_LOADS_PER_THREAD = (BK * BN) / NTHREADS;  // 4\n\nstatic_assert(A_LOADS_PER_THREAD == 4 && B_LOADS_PER_THREAD == 4, \"tile config\");\n\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(NTHREADS)\ndgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n            double* __restrict__ C, const size_t M, const size_t N, const size_t K) {\n    __shared__ double As[2][BK][BM + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[2][BK][BN];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * THREADS_X + tx;\n\n    const size_t blockRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * BM;\n    const size_t blockCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BN;\n\n    // A tile (BM rows x BK cols): each thread loads 4 consecutive k-elements of one row.\n    const int aRow = tid / (BK / 4);        // 0..63\n    const int aCol = (tid % (BK / 4)) * 4;  // 0,4,8,12\n    // B tile (BK rows x BN cols): each thread loads 4 consecutive n-elements of one row.\n    const int bRow = tid / (BN / 4);        // 0..15\n    const int bCol = (tid % (BN / 4)) * 4;  // 0..60\n\n    const size_t gARow = blockRow + aRow;\n    const size_t gBCol = blockCol + bCol;\n    const bool aRowOk = gARow < M;\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) acc[i][j] = 0.0;\n\n    double aReg[4], bReg[4];\n\n    auto loadGlobal = [&](size_t kBase) {\n        const size_t kA = kBase + aCol;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {\n            const size_t k = kA + t;\n            aReg[t] = (aRowOk && k < K) ? A[gARow * K + k] : 0.0;\n        }\n        const size_t kB = kBase + bRow;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {\n            const size_t n = gBCol + t;\n            bReg[t] = (kB < K && n < N) ? B[kB * N + n] : 0.0;\n        }\n    };\n\n    auto storeShared = [&](int buf) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) As[buf][aCol + t][aRow] = aReg[t];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) Bs[buf][bRow][bCol + t] = bReg[t];\n    };\n\n    const size_t numTiles = (K + BK - 1) / BK;\n\n    loadGlobal(0);\n    storeShared(0);\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < numTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(tile & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n\n        if (tile + 1 < numTiles) loadGlobal((tile + 1) * BK);\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double a[TM], b[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) a[i] = As[cur][k][ty * TM + i];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) b[j] = Bs[cur][k][tx * TN + j];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) acc[i][j] = fma(a[i], b[j], acc[i][j]);\n        }\n\n        if (tile + 1 < numTiles) {\n            storeShared(nxt);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Epilogue\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n        const size_t row = blockRow + ty * TM + i;\n        if (row >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n            const size_t col = blockCol + tx * TN + j;\n            if (col < N) C[row * N + col] = acc[i][j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GPU driver for one rank: localC[localRows x N] = localA[localRows x N] * B\n// Host buffers must be pinned. The row block is processed in chunks on two\n// streams so that H2D (A chunk) / kernel / D2H (C chunk) overlap.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nvoid matrixMultiplyGPU(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC,\n                       const size_t localRows, const size_t N) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || N == 0) return;\n\n    constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 2;\n    const size_t rowBytes = N * sizeof(double);\n\n    // Chunk size: keep per-chunk buffers around 256 MiB, aligned to BM rows.\n    size_t chunkRows = std::max<size_t>(BM, (256ull << 20) / rowBytes);\n    chunkRows = (chunkRows / BM) * BM;\n    chunkRows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows);\n    if (chunkRows == 0) chunkRows = localRows;\n\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* dC[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * rowBytes));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    }\n\n    // B is needed by every chunk: upload once on stream 0 and make stream 1 wait.\n    cudaEvent_t bReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&bReady, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(bReady, streams[0]));\n    for (int s = 1; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(streams[s], bReady, 0));\n\n    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);\n    size_t chunkIdx = 0;\n    for (size_t r0 = 0; r0 < localRows; r0 += chunkRows, ++chunkIdx) {\n        const int s = static_cast<int>(chunkIdx % NUM_STREAMS);\n        const size_t rows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows - r0);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN),\n                        static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA[s], localA + r0 * N, rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[s]));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, streams[s]>>>(dA[s], dB, dC[s], rows, N, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + r0 * N, dC[s], rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]));\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[s]));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(bReady));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC[s]));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validation: recompute a few elements on the host from the generator and\n// compare with the gathered C (rank 0 only). Same points/tolerance as original.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    bool valid = true;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n#pragma omp critical\n                {\n                    printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                           i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                    valid = false;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    bool exitEarly = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (all ranks see identical argv)\n    for (int i = 1;  // establish context now so it is not timed later\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"Parallel config: %d MPI ranks, %d OpenMP threads/rank, %d GPU(s)/node, %d ranks/node\\n\",\n               size, omp_get_max_threads(), deviceCount, localSize);\n    }\n\n    // ---- Row-block decomposition\n    const size_t rowsPerRank = N / size;\n    const size_t remainder = N % size;\n    const size_t localRows = rowsPerRank + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * rowsPerRank +\n                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);\n\n    // ---- Allocate (pinned host memory for async transfers)\n    double* localA = nullptr;\n    double* B = nullptr;\n    double* localC = nullptr;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * N;\n    const size_t fullElems = N * N;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localA, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&B, std::max<size_t>(fullElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localC, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n\n    // ---- Initialise matrices (OpenMP, each rank generates what it needs)\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(localA, N, rowBegin, localRows);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Gather layout (row datatype keeps counts small even for huge N)\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rowType));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_commit(&rowType));\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts, displs;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        recvCounts.resize(size);\n        displs.resize(size);\n        for (int r = 0; Every rank owns its A row block and a full copy of B\n//             (both generated locally from the deterministic generator, so no\n//             broadcast is needed). Row blocks of C are gathered to rank 0.\n//   * CUDA  : each rank drives one GPU (local rank -> device, round-robin).\n//             The local row block is processed in chunks on two streams so\n//             host<->device transfers overlap with the tiled DGEMM kernel.\n//   * OpenMP: multi-threaded matrix initialisation, result packing and\n//             host-side validation on every rank.\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                               \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                               \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,      \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                         \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        int err__ = (call); ++r) {\n        const size_t i = rowBegin + r;\n        double* row = mat + r * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA tiled DGEMM kernel: C[M x N] = A[M x K] * B[K x N] (row-major)\n// Block tile BM x BN, K-step BK, each thread computes a TM x TN micro tile.\n// Shared memory is double buffered and global loads are prefetched into\n// registers so that loads of tile t+1 overlap the FMAs of tile t.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int BM = 64;\nconstexpr int BN = 64;\nconstexpr int BK = 16;\nconstexpr int TM = 4;\nconstexpr int TN = 4;\nconstexpr int THREADS_X = BN / TN;  // 4\n\nstatic_assert(A_LOADS_PER_THREAD == 4 && B_LOADS_PER_THREAD == 4, \"tile config\");\n\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(NTHREADS)\ndgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n            double* __restrict__ C, const size_t M, const size_t N, const size_t K) {\n    __shared__ double As[2][BK][BM + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[2][BK][BN];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const int tid = ty * THREADS_X + tx;\n\n    const size_t blockRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * BM;\n    const size_t blockCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * BN;\n\n    // A tile (BM rows x BK cols): each thread loads 4 consecutive k-elements of one row.\n    const int aRow = tid / (BK / 4);        // 0..63\n    const int aCol = (tid % (BK / 4)) * 4;  // 0,4,8,12\n    // B tile (BK rows x BN cols): each thread loads 4 consecutive n-elements of one row.\n    const int bRow = tid / (BN / 4);        // 0..15\n    const int bCol = (tid % (BN / 4)) * 4;  // 0..60\n\n    const size_t gARow = blockRow + aRow;\n    const size_t gBCol = blockCol + bCol;\n    const bool aRowOk = gARow < M;\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) acc[i][j] = 0.0;\n\n    double aReg[4], bReg[4];\n\n    auto loadGlobal = [&](size_t kBase) {\n        const size_t kA = kBase + aCol;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {\n            const size_t k = kA + t;\n            aReg[t] = (aRowOk && k < K) ? A[gARow * K + k] : 0.0;\n        }\n        const size_t kB = kBase + bRow;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) {\n            const size_t n = gBCol + t;\n            bReg[t] = (kB < K && n < N) ? B[kB * N + n] : 0.0;\n        }\n    };\n\n    auto storeShared = [&](int buf) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) As[buf][aCol + t][aRow] = aReg[t];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; ++t) Bs[buf][bRow][bCol + t] = bReg[t];\n    };\n\n    const size_t numTiles = (K + BK - 1) / BK;\n\n    loadGlobal(0);\n    storeShared(0);\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < numTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(tile & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n\n        if (tile + 1 < numTiles) loadGlobal((tile + 1) * BK);\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double a[TM], b[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) a[i] = As[cur][k][ty * TM + i];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) b[j] = Bs[cur][k][tx * TN + j];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) acc[i][j] = fma(a[i], b[j], acc[i][j]);\n        }\n\n        if (tile + 1 < numTiles) {\n            storeShared(nxt);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Epilogue\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = 0; i < TM; ++i) {\n        const size_t row = blockRow + ty * TM + i;\n        if (row >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < TN; ++j) {\n            const size_t col = blockCol + tx * TN + j;\n            if (col < N) C[row * N + col] = acc[i][j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GPU driver for one rank: localC[localRows x N] = localA[localRows x N] * B\n// Host buffers must be pinned. The row block is processed in chunks on two\n// streams so that H2D (A chunk) / kernel / D2H (C chunk) overlap.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nvoid matrixMultiplyGPU(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC,\n                       const size_t localRows, const size_t N) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || N == 0) return;\n\n    constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 2;\n    const size_t rowBytes = N * sizeof(double);\n\n    // Chunk size: keep per-chunk buffers around 256 MiB, aligned to BM rows.\n    size_t chunkRows = std::max<size_t>(BM, (256ull << 20) / rowBytes);\n    chunkRows = (chunkRows / BM) * BM;\n    chunkRows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows);\n    if (chunkRows == 0) chunkRows = localRows;\n\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* dC[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * rowBytes));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    }\n\n    // B is needed by every chunk: upload once on stream 0 and make stream 1 wait.\n    cudaEvent_t bReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&bReady, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(bReady, streams[0]));\n    for (int s = 1; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(streams[s], bReady, 0));\n\n    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);\n    size_t chunkIdx = 0;\n    for (size_t r0 = 0; r0 < localRows; r0 += chunkRows, ++chunkIdx) {\n        const int s = static_cast<int>(chunkIdx % NUM_STREAMS);\n        const size_t rows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows - r0);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN),\n                        static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA[s], localA + r0 * N, rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[s]));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, streams[s]>>>(dA[s], dB, dC[s], rows, N, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + r0 * N, dC[s], rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]));\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[s]));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(bReady));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC[s]));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validation: recompute a few elements on the host from the generator and\n// compare with the gathered C (rank 0 only). Same points/tolerance as original.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    bool valid = true;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n#pragma omp critical\n                {\n                    printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                           i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                    valid = false;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    bool exitEarly = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (all ranks see identical argv)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            exitEarly = true;\n            exitCode = 0;\n            break;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            exitEarly = true;\n            exitCode = 1;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    if (exitEarly) {\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return exitCode;\n    }\n\n    // ---- GPU selection: one device per rank, round-robin over node-local ranks\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));  // establish context now so it is not timed later\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"Parallel config: %d MPI ranks, %d OpenMP threads/rank, %d GPU(s)/node, %d ranks/node\\n\",\n               size, omp_get_max_threads(), deviceCount, localSize);\n    }\n\n    // ---- Row-block decomposition\n    const size_t rowsPerRank = N / size;\n    const size_t remainder = N % size;\n    const size_t localRows = rowsPerRank + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * rowsPerRank +\n                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);\n\n    // ---- Allocate (pinned host memory for async transfers)\n    double* localA = nullptr;\n    double* B = nullptr;\n    double* localC = nullptr;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * N;\n    const size_t fullElems = N * N;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localA, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&B, std::max<size_t>(fullElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localC, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n\n    // ---- Initialise matrices (OpenMP, each rank generates what it needs)\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(localA, N, rowBegin, localRows);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Gather layout (row datatype keeps counts small even for huge N)\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rowType));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_commit(&rowType));\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts, displs;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        recvCounts.resize(size);\n        displs.resize(size);\n        for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n            const size_t rr = rowsPerRank + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t rb = static_cast<size_t>(r) * rowsPerRank + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);\n            recvCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(rr);\n            displs[r] = static_cast<int>(rb);\n        }\n    }\n    std::vector<double> C;\n    if (rank == 0) C.resize(fullElems);\n\n    // ---- Compute\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    matrixMultiplyGPU(localA, B, localC, localRows, N);\n\n    // Assemble the full result on rank 0\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Gatherv(localC, static_cast<int>(localRows), rowType,\n                          rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr,\n                          rank == 0 ? recvCounts.data() : nullptr,\n                          rank == 0 ? displs.data() : nullptr,\n                          rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_free(&rowType));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", static_cast<long>(duration.count()));\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(C, N);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 0;\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(B));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localC));\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":3,"newStart":1,"newLines":21,"lines":["+// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA matrix multiplication benchmark.","+//","+// Decomposition:","+//   * MPI   : C = A * B is partitioned by contiguous row blocks of A / C across","+//             ranks. Every rank (including the receiver) derives\n// the same layout from (localRows, N), so per-chunk messages can be matched.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nsize_t chunkRowsFor(const size_t localRows, const size_t N) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || N == 0) return 0;\n    const size_t rowBytes = N * sizeof(double);\n    // Keep per-chunk buffers around 128 MiB, aligned to BM rows.\n    size_t chunkRows = std::max<size_t>(BM, (128ull << 20) / rowBytes);\n    chunkRows = (chunkRows / BM) * BM;\n    chunkRows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows);\n    return chunkRows == 0 ? localRows : chunkRows;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GPU driver for one rank: localC[localRows x N] = localA[localRows x N] * B\n// Host buffers must be pinned. The row block is processed in chunks on two\n// streams so that H2D (A chunk) / kernel / D2H (C chunk) overlap. As soon as a\n// chunk has landed in host memory, `onChunkDone(rowOffset, rows)` is invoked\n// (in chunk order) so the caller can ship it off while the GPU keeps working.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ntemplate <typename ChunkCallback>\nvoid matrixMultiplyGPU(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC,\n                       const size_t localRows, const size_t N, ChunkCallback&& onChunkDone) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || N == 0) return;\n\n    constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 2;\n    const size_t rowBytes = N * sizeof(double);\n    const size_t chunkRows = chunkRowsFor(localRows, N);\n    const size_t numChunks = (localRows + chunkRows - 1) / chunkRows;\n\n    double* dB = nullptr;\n    double* dA[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* dC[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * rowBytes));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC[s], chunkRows * rowBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&streams[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    }\n\n    // B is needed by every chunk: upload once on stream 0 and make stream 1 wait.\n    cudaEvent_t bReady;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&bReady, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(bReady, streams[0]));\n    for (int s = 1; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(streams[s], bReady, 0));\n\n    std::vector<cudaEvent_t> chunkDone(numChunks);\n    for (auto& e : chunkDone) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&e, cudaEventDisableTiming));\n\n    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        const int s = static_cast<int>(c % NUM_STREAMS);\n        const size_t r0 = c * chunkRows;\n        const size_t rows = std::min(chunkRows, localRows - r0);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN),\n                        static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA[s], localA + r0 * N, rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams[s]));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, streams[s]>>>(dA[s], dB, dC[s], rows, N, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + r0 * N, dC[s], rows * rowBytes,\n                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(chunkDone[c], streams[s]));\n    }\n\n    // Hand chunks to the caller as they complete (in order) while later chunks run.\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < numChunks; ++c) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(chunkDone[c]));\n        const size_t r0 = c * chunkRows;\n        onChunkDone(r0, std::min(chunkRows, localRows - r0));\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[s]));\n\n    for (auto& e : chunkDone) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(e));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(bReady));\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC[s]));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n}\n\n'''\ns = s[:old_start] + new + s[old_end:]\n\n# 2. main: allocation of localC / C, gather replaced by per-chunk Isend/Irecv\nold = s[s.index('    // ---- Allocate (pinned host memory for async transfers)'):s.index('    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();')]\nnew = r'''    // ---- Allocate (pinned host memory for async transfers)\n    // Rank 0 computes its own row block directly into the full result matrix C\n    // (its block starts at row 0); other ranks use a pinned local buffer.\n    double* localA = nullptr;\n    double* B = nullptr;\n    double* localC = nullptr;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * N;\n    const size_t fullElems = N * N;\n    std::vector<double> C;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localA, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&B, std::max<size_t>(fullElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        C.resize(fullElems);\n        if (localElems > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostRegister(C.data(), localElems * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterDefault));\n        }\n        localC = C.data();\n    } else {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&localC, std::max<size_t>(localElems, 1) * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Initialise matrices (OpenMP, each rank generates what it needs)\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(localA, N, rowBegin, localRows);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Row datatype keeps MPI counts small even for huge N.\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(N), MPI_DOUBLE, &rowType));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Type_commit(&rowType));\n\n    // ---- Compute\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Rank 0 pre-posts one receive per (rank, chunk) straight into C so that\n    // remote chunks stream in while all GPUs are still busy.\n    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 1; r < size; ++r) {\n            const size_t rr = rowsPerRank + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t rb = static_cast<size_t>(r) * rowsPerRank + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);\n            const size_t cr = chunkRowsFor(rr, N);\n            for (size_t r0 = 0; r0 < rr; r0 += cr) {\n                const size_t rows = std::min(cr, rr - r0);\n                requests.emplace_back();\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(C.data() + (rb + r0) * N, static_cast<int>(rows), rowType,\n                                    r, static_cast<int>(r0 / cr), MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests.back()));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t myChunkRows = chunkRowsFor(localRows, N);\n    matrixMultiplyGPU(localA, B, localC, localRows, N, [&](size_t r0, size_t rows) {\n        if (rank != 0) {\n            requests.emplace_back();\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Isend(localC + r0 * N, static_cast<int>(rows), rowType, 0,\n                                static_cast<int>(r0 / myChunkRows), MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests.back()));\n        }\n    });\n\n    if (!requests.empty()) {\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests.size()), requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));\n    }\n\n'''\ns = s.replace(old, new)\n\nold_free = '''    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(B));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localC));\n'''\nnew_free = '''    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(B));\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        if (localElems > 0) CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostUnregister(C.data()));\n    } else {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(localC));\n    }\n'''\nassert old_free in s\ns = s.replace(old_free, new_free)\n# update header comment\ns = s.replace(\"(both generated locally from the deterministic generator, so no\\n//             broadcast is needed). {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CfwVBKgm8MuWEZjdc4jNW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The matmul benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, built and verified on this machine. Only the two existing files changed, and the executable is still `matmul`.\n\n**What the parallelization does**\n\n- **MPI** splits A and C into contiguous row blocks, one per rank. Every rank generates its own A rows and a full B from the deterministic generator, so nothing is broadcast. Each rank maps to one GPU by node-local rank.\n- **CUDA** runs a hand-written tiled DGEMM kernel (64×64 block tile, 4×4 register micro-tile, double-buffered shared memory with register prefetch). Each rank processes its block in chunks on two streams so host-to-device, compute, and device-to-host transfers overlap. No cuBLAS, so no new dependencies.\n- **OpenMP** parallelizes matrix initialization on every rank and the validation checks on rank 0.\n- **Result assembly** is overlapped with compute. Finished chunks are sent to rank 0 with non-blocking sends while GPUs are still busy, and rank 0 computes its own block directly into the final matrix.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nBuilt-in validation passes for every size and rank count tested (1, 7, 100, 512, 1000, 4096 with 1, 3, and 4 ranks). Results are bit-identical to the original for sizes where products are exact (powers of two). For other sizes they differ by at most one ULP because the GPU uses fused multiply-add throughout. The original binary itself produces a third bit pattern at those sizes from gcc's partial FMA contraction under `-march=native`, so no single exact hash exists. Help, unknown-option, and N=0 behave as before.\n\n**Performance** (this host, 4× RTX 3090, timing includes all transfers and result assembly)\n\n| N | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 8192 | 606 GFLOPS | 960 GFLOPS | 1387 GFLOPS |\n| 16384 | 623 GFLOPS | 1039 GFLOPS | 1761 GFLOPS |\n\nSingle-GPU throughput is at the RTX 3090's FP64 peak. The 4-GPU scaling gap comes from each rank uploading a full copy of B and the result streaming into rank 0 over PCIe.\n\n**Build and run**\n\n```\ncmake -S matmul -B build && cmake --build build -j\nmpirun --bind-to none -np 4 ./build/matmul -n 8192 -v\n```\n\nThe CMake file now requires MPI, OpenMP, and the CUDA toolkit, compiles the source with nvcc, and targets the GPU architecture detected at configure time. Use `--bind-to none` or an equivalent so Open MPI does not pin each rank's OpenMP threads to a single core."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":397,"cache_read_input_tokens":63716,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":397},"output_tokens":795,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"05e4c95a-212b-4511-b2dd-b22a360ae215","uuid":"7451c1a5-044e-4be2-b0f7-61022002379a","timestamp":"2026-10-12T01:37:13.997Z","request_id":"req_011CfwVBKQAFPKcxTqNzTGjs"}

Every rank generates its own A rows and a full B from the deterministic generator, so nothing is broadcast. Each rank maps to one GPU by node-local rank.\n- **CUDA** runs a hand-written tiled DGEMM kernel (64×64 block tile, 4×4 register micro-tile, double-buffered shared memory with register prefetch). Each rank processes its block in chunks on two streams so host-to-device, compute, and device-to-host transfers overlap. No cuBLAS, so no new dependencies.\n- **OpenMP** parallelizes matrix initialization on every rank and the validation checks on rank 0.\n- **Result assembly** is overlapped with compute. Finished chunks are sent to rank 0 with non-blocking sends while GPUs are still busy, and rank 0 computes its own block directly into the final matrix.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nBuilt-in validation passes for every size and rank count tested (1, 7, 100, 512, 1000, 4096 with 1, 3, and 4 ranks). Results are bit-identical to the original for sizes where products are exact (powers of two). For other sizes they differ by at most one ULP because the GPU uses fused multiply-add throughout. The original binary itself produces a third bit pattern at those sizes from gcc's partial FMA contraction under `-march=native`, so no single exact hash exists. Help, unknown-option, and N=0 behave as before.\n\n**Performance** (this host, 4× RTX 3090, timing includes all transfers and result assembly)\n\n| N | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 8192 | 606 GFLOPS | 960 GFLOPS | 1387 GFLOPS |\n| 16384 | 623 GFLOPS | 1039 GFLOPS | 1761 GFLOPS |\n\nSingle-GPU throughput is at the RTX 3090's FP64 peak. The 4-GPU scaling gap comes from each rank uploading a full copy of B and the result streaming into rank 0 over PCIe.\n\n**Build and run**\n\n```\ncmake -S matmul -B build && cmake --build build -j\nmpirun --bind-to none -np 4 ./build/matmul -n 8192 -v\n```\n\nThe CMake file now requires MPI, OpenMP, and the CUDA toolkit, compiles the source with nvcc, and targets the GPU architecture detected at configure time. Use `--bind-to none` or an equivalent so Open MPI does not pin each rank's OpenMP threads to a single core.","ttft_ms":1294,"type":"result","duration_ms":739179,"uuid":"a1dca953-6846-49ea-aab9-3ca7b8c8a69b","ttft_stream_ms":1158,"time_to_request_ms":138,"first_content_frame_ms":1158,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
